33:   String _getInitials(String name) {
34:     if (name.isEmpty) return "";
35:     final parts = name.trim().split(RegExp(r'\s+'));
36:     if (parts.length >= 2) {
37:       return (parts[0][0] + parts[1][0]).toUpperCase();
38:     } else if (parts[0].length >= 2) {
39:       return parts[0].substring(0, 2).toUpperCase();
40:     } else {
41:       return parts[0][0].toUpperCase();
42:     }
43:   }

38:   String _getInitials(String name) {
39:     if (name.isEmpty) return "";
40:     final parts = name.trim().split(RegExp(r'\s+'));
41:     if (parts.length >= 2) {
42:       return (parts[0][0] + parts[1][0]).toUpperCase();
43:     } else if (parts[0].length >= 2) {
44:       return parts[0].substring(0, 2).toUpperCase();
45:     } else {
46:       return parts[0][0].toUpperCase();
47:     }
48:   }

57:   Future<void> _saveMessages() async {
58:     try {
59:       final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
60:       final String jsonStr = json.encode(_messages);
61:       await prefs.setString('chat_messages_${widget.name}', jsonStr);
62:     } catch (e) {
63:       debugPrint("Error saving messages: $e");
64:     }
65:   }
66: 
67:   String _getInitials(String name) {
68:     if (name.isEmpty) return "";
69:     final parts = name.trim().split(RegExp(r'\s+'));
70:     if (parts.length >= 2) {
71:       return (parts[0][0] + parts[1][0]).toUpperCase();
72:     } else if (parts[0].length >= 2) {
73:       return parts[0].substring(0, 2).toUpperCase();
74:     } else {
75:       return parts[0][0].toUpperCase();
76:     }
77:   }

305:   String _getInitials(String name) {
306:     if (name.isEmpty) return "";
307:     final parts = name.trim().split(RegExp(r'\s+'));
308:     if (parts.length >= 2) {
309:       return (parts[0][0] + parts[1][0]).toUpperCase();
310:     } else if (parts[0].length >= 2) {
311:       return parts[0].substring(0, 2).toUpperCase();
312:     } else {
313:       return parts[0][0].toUpperCase();
314:     }
315:   }

105:   void initState() {

107:     _currentAvatarUrl = widget.avatarUrl;
108:     _loadSavedMessages();

111:   Future<void> _loadSavedMessages() async {
112:     try {
113:       final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
114:       final String? jsonStr = prefs.getString('chat_messages_${widget.name}');
115:       if (jsonStr != null) {
116:         final List<dynamic> decoded = json.decode(jsonStr);
117:         setState(() {
118:           _messages.clear();
119:           _messages.addAll(decoded.map((m) => Map<String, dynamic>.from(m)));
120:         });
121:       }
122:     } catch (e) {
123:       debugPrint("Error loading messages: $e");
124:     }
125:   }
126: 
127:   Future<void> _saveMessages() async {
128:     try {
129:       final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
130:       final String jsonStr = json.encode(_messages);
131:       await prefs.setString('chat_messages_${widget.name}', jsonStr);
132:     } catch (e) {
133:       debugPrint("Error saving messages: $e");
134:     }
135:   }
